    assert "shim-x64" not in _entries(sections)


def test_instalador_se_puede_desactivar():
    with_installer = parse_packages_from_kickstart(
        DesktopKickstartGenerator(_config(enable_installer=True)).render()
    )
    without_installer = parse_packages_from_kickstart(
        DesktopKickstartGenerator(_config(enable_installer=False)).render()
    )
    assert "anaconda" in with_installer
    assert "anaconda" not in without_installer


def test_ssh_solo_se_configura_cuando_se_habilita():
    disabled = DesktopKickstartGenerator(_config(enable_ssh=False)).render()
    enabled = DesktopKickstartGenerator(_config(enable_ssh=True)).render()

    assert "00-creador-desktop.conf" not in disabled
    assert "sshd" not in disabled.split("%packages", 1)[1].split("%end", 1)[0]
    assert "00-creador-desktop.conf" in enabled
    assert "sshd" in enabled


def test_firewall_se_puede_desactivar():

        _config(enable_firewall=False)
    ).render()

    assert "firewall --disabled" in kickstart
    assert "firewalld" not in packages
    assert "systemctl enable firewalld.service" not in kickstart


def test_hash_de_contrasena_invalido_se_rechaza():

        DesktopKickstartGenerator(config).render()


def test_usuario_invalido_se_rechaza():

            DesktopKickstartGenerator(_config(desktop_user=username)).render()

    with pytest.raises(DesktopKickstartError):

            _config(product_name='Producto"$(rm -rf /)')
        ).render()


def test_write_es_atomico_y_privado(tmp_path):
    target = tmp_path / "desktop.ks"
    result = DesktopKickstartGenerator(_config()).write(target)
    assert result == str(target)
    mode = stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(target).st_mode)
    assert mode == 0o600
    assert "%packages" in target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert not list(tmp_path.glob(".desktop-kickstart-*"))

    )
    packages = builder._package_lines(builder._resolve_app())
    assert "cage" in packages
    assert "seatd" in packages

==> ./tests/test_dnf_query.py <==
"""Pruebas del adaptador de consultas DNF, sin red ni DNF real."""

import json
import subprocess

import pytest

from core.repositories.dnf_query import (
    DnfQuery,
    DnfQueryError,
    DnfUnavailableError,
)
from core.software.policy import SoftwareSelectionError


class FakeResult:
    def __init__(self, stdout="", stderr="", returncode=0):
        self.stdout = stdout
        self.stderr = stderr
        self.returncode = returncode


class Recorder:
    def __init__(self, outcomes):
        self.outcomes = list(outcomes)
        self.calls = []

    def __call__(self, command, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append((command, kwargs))
        outcome = self.outcomes.pop(0)
        if isinstance(outcome, BaseException):
            raise outcome
        return outcome


def _query(outcomes):
    recorder = Recorder(outcomes)
    query = DnfQuery(
        dnf_binary="/usr/bin/dnf",
        runner=recorder,
        which=lambda _name: None,
    )
    return query, recorder


def test_available_repositories_json_is_parsed_and_sorted():
    payload = [
        {"id": "updates", "name": "Fedora Updates", "is_enabled": True},
        {"id": "fedora", "name": "Fedora 44", "is_enabled": True},
        {"id": "disabled", "name": "No usar", "is_enabled": False},
    ]
    query, recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout=json.dumps(payload))])
    repositories = query.list_enabled_repositories()

    assert [repo.id for repo in repositories] == ["fedora", "updates"]
    assert repositories[0].name == "Fedora 44"
    command, kwargs = recorder.calls[0]
    assert command == ["/usr/bin/dnf", "-q", "repolist", "--enabled", "--json"]
    assert kwargs.get("shell", False) is False
    assert kwargs["stdin"] == subprocess.DEVNULL
    assert "LC_ALL" in kwargs["env"]
    assert kwargs["env"]["LC_ALL"] == "C.UTF-8"


def test_repository_text_output_is_used_when_json_is_not_supported():
    text = (
        "repo id                   repo name                     status\n"
        "fedora                    Fedora 44 - x86_64            70000\n"
        "updates                   Fedora 44 - x86_64 - Updates  1200\n"
    )
    query, recorder = _query(
        [
            FakeResult(stderr="opción --json desconocida", returncode=2),
            FakeResult(stdout=text),
        ]
    )
    repositories = query.list_enabled_repositories()

    assert [repo.id for repo in repositories] == ["fedora", "updates"]
    assert [repo.name for repo in repositories] == [
        "Fedora 44 - x86_64",
        "Fedora 44 - x86_64 - Updates",
    ]
    assert len(recorder.calls) == 2


def test_invalid_repository_json_falls_back_to_text():
    text = "fedora    Fedora 44\n"
    query, recorder = _query(
        [FakeResult(stdout="{no es json"), FakeResult(stdout=text)]
    )
    repositories = query.list_enabled_repositories()

    assert [repo.id for repo in repositories] == ["fedora"]
    assert len(recorder.calls) == 2


def test_package_search_uses_separated_query_and_parses_results():
    output = (
        "Matched fields: name (exact)\n"
        " htop.x86_64\tInteractive process viewer\n"
        " pcp-htop.x86_64\tPerformance Co-Pilot interactive process viewer\n"
        " htop.x86_64\tDuplicado\n"
        " bad;name.x86_64\tEntrada inválida\n"
    )
    query, recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout=output)])
    packages = query.search_packages("htop", limit=10)

    assert [(pkg.name, pkg.summary) for pkg in packages] == [
        ("htop", "Interactive process viewer"),
        ("pcp-htop", "Performance Co-Pilot interactive process viewer"),
    ]
    command, kwargs = recorder.calls[0]
    assert command == [
        "/usr/bin/dnf",
        "-q",
        "search",
        "--all",
        "--",
        "htop",
    ]
    assert kwargs.get("shell", False) is False


def test_full_catalog_uses_repoquery_and_parses_repository_details():
    output = (
        "git\tFast Version Control System\tFedora 44 - x86_64\t2.56.1\n"
        "htop\tInteractive process viewer\tFedora 44 - x86_64\t3.4.1\n"
        "git\tVersión duplicada\tOtro repo\t2.0.0\n"
    )
    query, recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout=output)])
    packages = query.search_packages("", limit=1)

    assert len(packages) == 1
    assert packages[0].name == "git"
    assert packages[0].repository == "Fedora 44 - x86_64"
    assert packages[0].version == "2.56.1"
    command, _kwargs = recorder.calls[0]
    assert command[2] == "repoquery"
    assert "--latest-limit=1" in command


def test_search_query_is_validated_before_running_dnf():
    query, recorder = _query([])
    for value in ("-peligro", "linea\nnueva", "x" * 121):
        with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError):
            query.search_packages(value)
    assert recorder.calls == []


def test_result_limit_is_validated():
    query, recorder = _query([])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        query.search_packages("htop", limit=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        query.search_packages("htop", limit="muchos")
    assert recorder.calls == []


def test_groups_are_parsed_sorted_and_deduplicated():
    output = (
        "ID                          Name                                        Installed\n"
        "office                      Office/Productivity                         no\n"
        "multimedia                  Multimedia                                  yes\n"
        "office                      Duplicado                                   no\n"
        "bad id                      Inválido                                    no\n"
    )
    query, recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout=output)])
    groups = query.list_groups()

    assert [(group.id, group.name, group.installed) for group in groups] == [
        ("multimedia", "Multimedia", True),
        ("office", "Office/Productivity", False),
    ]
    assert recorder.calls[0][0] == [
        "/usr/bin/dnf",
        "-q",
        "group",
        "list",
        "--hidden",
    ]


def test_group_info_is_parsed_with_all_package_sections():
    output = (
        "Id                   : office\n"
        "Name                 : Office/Productivity\n"
        "Description          : Suite de oficina\n"
        "Installed            : no\n"
        "Repositories         : fedora, updates\n"
        "Mandatory packages   : libreoffice-core\n"
        "Default packages     : libreoffice-writer\n"
        "                     : libreoffice-calc\n"
        "Optional packages    : abiword\n"
        "Conditional packages : paquete-cond if otro-paquete\n"
    )
    query, recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout=output)])
    details = query.group_info("@office")

    assert details is not None
    assert details.id == "office"
    assert details.name == "Office/Productivity"
    assert details.repositories == ("fedora", "updates")
    assert details.mandatory == ("libreoffice-core",)
    assert details.default == ("libreoffice-writer", "libreoffice-calc")
    assert details.optional == ("abiword",)
    assert details.conditional == ("paquete-cond",)
    assert recorder.calls[0][0] == [
        "/usr/bin/dnf",
        "-q",
        "group",
        "info",
        "office",
    ]


def test_group_info_returns_none_when_group_does_not_exist():
    query, _recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout="")])
    assert query.group_info("no-existe") is None


def test_invalid_group_id_is_rejected_before_running_dnf():
    query, recorder = _query([])
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError):
        query.group_info("@grupo con espacios")
    assert recorder.calls == []


def test_missing_dnf_raises_unavailable_error():
    query = DnfQuery(which=lambda _name: None)
    assert query.available() is False
    with pytest.raises(DnfUnavailableError, match="dnf"):
        query.list_groups()


def test_dnf_failure_is_sanitized_and_truncated():
    query, _recorder = _query(
        [
            FakeResult(
                stderr="\x1b[31mError\x1b[0m\n" + "detalle " * 500,
                returncode=1,
            )
        ]
    )
    with pytest.raises(DnfQueryError) as error:
        query.list_groups()
    message = str(error.value)
    assert "\x1b" not in message
    assert "\n" not in message
    assert len(message) < 1000


def test_timeout_is_reported_as_query_error():
    query, _recorder = _query(
        [subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=["dnf"], timeout=1)]
    )
    with pytest.raises(DnfQueryError, match="180 segundos"):
        query.list_groups()


def test_constructor_rejects_invalid_timeout():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        DnfQuery(timeout=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        DnfQuery(timeout="mucho")


def test_multi_term_search_passes_each_term_after_separator():
    query, recorder = _query([FakeResult(stdout="")])
    query.search_packages("servidor web")
    command, _kwargs = recorder.calls[0]
    assert command == [
        "/usr/bin/dnf",
        "-q",
        "search",
        "--all",
        "--",
        "servidor",
        "web",
    ]

==> ./tests/test_gui_smoke.py <==
"""Pruebas de humo de la interfaz 2.0 con Qt offscreen."""

import os

os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen")

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from core.repositories.dnf_query import DnfGroup, DnfRepository
from main import KioskBuilderGUI, STEP_KEYS


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def app():
    return QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])


@pytest.fixture
def window(app, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", str(tmp_path / "config"))
    widget = KioskBuilderGUI()
    widget.show()
    app.processEvents()
    yield widget
    widget.close()
    app.processEvents()


def test_window_has_dashboard_six_steps_and_usb(window, app):
    expected = {
        "dashboard",
        "custom_distro",
        "step1",
        "step2",
        "step3",
        "step4",
        "step5",
        "step6",
        "usb",
    }
    assert set(window._pages) == expected
    # custom_distro se abre desde el panel y no tiene botón propio en la barra.
    assert len(window._nav_buttons) == 8
    assert window.stack.count() == 9
    assert window.combo_ui_mode.count() == 2
    assert window.combo_theme.count() == 2
    assert window._navigate("step1", validate=False)
    app.processEvents()
    assert window.stack.currentWidget() is window._pages["step1"]["widget"]
    assert window.lbl_breadcrumb.text() == "Asistente · Paso 1 de 6"


def test_theme_switch_changes_stylesheet(window):
    window._apply_theme("dark")
    assert "#0f1722" in window.styleSheet()
    assert window.theme_name == "dark"
    window._apply_theme("light")
    assert "#f3f6fb" in window.styleSheet()
    assert window.theme_name == "light"


def test_simple_mode_hides_advanced_options_and_resets_them(window, app):
    window._navigate("step1", validate=False)
    window._apply_ui_mode("advanced", confirm=False)
    app.processEvents()
    assert window.combo_base.isVisible()
    assert window.combo_boot.isVisible()

    window.check_multi_app.setChecked(True)
    window._apply_ui_mode("simple", confirm=False)
    app.processEvents()
    assert window.ui_mode == "simple"
    assert not window.combo_base.isVisible()
    assert not window.combo_boot.isVisible()
    assert not window.check_multi_app.isChecked()


def test_wizard_blocks_progress_with_invalid_step(window):
    shown = []
    window._show_issues = lambda issues: shown.extend(issues)
    window.txt_password.setText("")
    assert window._navigate("step1", validate=False)
    assert window._navigate("step2", validate=True)
    assert shown
    assert window._current_key == "step1"

    window.txt_password.setText("clave-segura-123")
    assert window._navigate("step2", validate=True)
    assert window._current_key == "step2"


def test_step_indicators_mark_complete_steps(window):
    window.txt_password.setText("clave-segura-123")
    window._refresh_step_indicators()
    assert window._nav_buttons["step1"].text().startswith("✓")


def test_help_panel_matches_current_page(window, app):
    window._navigate("step3", validate=False)
    window._on_help()
    app.processEvents()
    assert window.help_panel.isVisible()

    assert not window.help_panel.isVisible()

def test_dashboard_hashes_existing_iso_in_background(window, app, tmp_path):
    import hashlib

    iso = tmp_path / "Prueba.iso"
    payload = b"contenido-de-prueba" * 1024
    iso.write_bytes(payload)
    expected = hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()

    window._settings.setValue("last_iso_path", str(iso))
    window._settings.setValue("last_iso_size", iso.stat().st_size)
    window._settings.setValue("last_iso_mtime", int(iso.stat().st_mtime))
    window._settings.setValue("last_iso_sha256", "")
    window._settings.setValue("last_build_status", "ok")

    window.refresh_dashboard()
    assert window.hash_worker is not None
    assert window.hash_worker.wait(5000)
    for _ in range(5):
        app.processEvents()

    assert expected in window.card_hash.detail.text()
    assert window._settings.value("last_iso_sha256") == expected

    window.apply_custom_distro_selection()
    app.processEvents()

    assert window.collect_config()["extra_groups"] == "@office"

"""Pruebas de la validacion por etapas del asistente 2.0."""

import shutil
from collections import namedtuple

from gui import validation


Usage = namedtuple("Usage", "total used free")


def _config(tmp_path, **overrides):
    config = {
        "iso_name": "TallerArgOS-kiosk",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "developer": "Sergio",
        "product_name": "TallerArgOS Fedora Kiosk",
        "password": "clave-segura-123",
        "boot_mode": "dual",
        "app_mode": "chromium",
        "app_url": "http://localhost",
        "app_path": "",
        "multi_app": False,
        "app2_mode": "chromium",
        "app2_url": "",
        "app2_command": "",
        "app2_path": "",
        "extra_packages": "",
        "plymouth_image": "",
        "installer_banner": "",
        "enable_ssh": False,
        "ssh_password_auth": False,
        "output_dir": str(tmp_path / "ISOs"),
    }
    config.update(overrides)
    return config


def _fake_environment(monkeypatch, free=20 * 1024 ** 3):
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "gui.status.check_dependencies",
        lambda: {
            "ok": True,
            "missing": [],
            "available": 7,
            "total": 7,
            "tools": [],
            "assets": [],
        },
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        shutil, "disk_usage", lambda path: Usage(100 * 1024 ** 3, 0, free)
    )


def test_valid_configuration_has_no_issues(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    _fake_environment(monkeypatch)
    assert validation.validate_all(_config(tmp_path)) == []


def test_identity_rejects_control_characters_and_invalid_names(tmp_path):
    base = _config(tmp_path, project_type="office")
    issues = validation.validate_identity(
        dict(
            base,
            password="clave\ninyectada",
            iso_name="con espacio",
            version="-1",
            developer="x" * 81,
        )
    )
    messages = " ".join(issue.message for issue in issues)
    titles = " ".join(issue.title for issue in issues)
    assert "128 caracteres" in messages
    assert "Nombre de ISO" in titles
    assert "Version" in titles
    assert "Creador" in titles
    assert {issue.step for issue in issues} == {1}


def test_desktop_identity_accepts_short_initial_password(tmp_path):
    assert validation.validate_identity(
        _config(tmp_path, project_type="office", password="x")
    ) == []


def test_kiosk_identity_does_not_require_password(tmp_path):
    assert validation.validate_identity(_config(tmp_path, password="")) == []


def test_desktop_identity_requires_valid_password(tmp_path):
    for password in ("", "x" * 129):
        issues = validation.validate_identity(
            _config(tmp_path, project_type="office", password=password)
        )
        assert any("Contrasena inicial" in issue.title for issue in issues)


def test_desktop_identity_accepts_short_initial_password(tmp_path):
    assert validation.validate_identity(
        _config(tmp_path, project_type="office", password="x")
    ) == []


def test_kiosk_identity_does_not_require_password(tmp_path):
    assert validation.validate_identity(_config(tmp_path, password="")) == []


def test_desktop_identity_requires_valid_password(tmp_path):

        )

    assert any("producto" in issue.title for issue in issues)


def test_appearance_checks_images_only_when_present(tmp_path):
    base = _config(tmp_path)
    assert validation.validate_appearance(base) == []

    missing = tmp_path / "no-existe.png"
    issues = validation.validate_appearance(dict(base, plymouth_image=str(missing)))
    assert any(issue.step == 2 and "Falta" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    big = tmp_path / "grande.png"
    big.write_bytes(b"0")
    with big.open("r+b") as handle:
        handle.truncate(4 * 1024 ** 2 + 1)
    issues = validation.validate_appearance(dict(base, plymouth_image=str(big)))
    assert any("demasiado grande" in issue.title for issue in issues)



def test_software_validates_local_apps_packages_and_second_window(tmp_path):
    base = _config(tmp_path)
    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, app_mode="custom", app_path=str(tmp_path / "no-existe"))
    )
    assert any("Falta la aplicacion" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, extra_packages="htop;rm -rf /")
    )
    assert any("Paquetes no validos" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, extra_packages="telnet")
    )
    assert any("Paquetes bloqueados" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, extra_groups="@office grupo-con-espacios")
    )
    assert any("Grupos no validos" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, multi_app=True, app2_mode="firefox", app2_url="")
    )
    assert any("segunda app" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, multi_app=True, app2_mode="custom", app2_command="a\nb")
    )
    assert any("Comando no valido" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, multi_app=True, app2_mode="local", app2_path="")
    )
    assert any("Falta la segunda aplicacion" in issue.title for issue in issues)


def test_software_desktop_no_exige_aplicacion_de_kiosco(tmp_path):

        tmp_path,
        engine="desktop",
        desktop_environment="gnome",

    issues = validation.validate_software(dict(base, desktop_user="1usuario"))
    assert any("Usuario de escritorio" in issue.title for issue in issues)

    issues = validation.validate_software(
        dict(base, extra_packages="telnet")
    )
    assert any("bloqueados" in issue.title for issue in issues)

def test_build_reports_missing_dependencies_and_low_space(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "gui.status.check_dependencies",
        lambda: {
            "ok": False,
            "missing": [{"name": "xorrisofs", "package": "xorriso"}],
            "available": 6,
            "total": 7,
            "tools": [],
            "assets": [],
        },
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        shutil, "disk_usage", lambda path: Usage(100 * 1024 ** 3, 99 * 1024 ** 3, 1024)
    )
    issues = validation.validate_build(_config(tmp_path))
    messages = " ".join(issue.message for issue in issues)
    titles = " ".join(issue.title for issue in issues)

    assert "Espacio insuficiente" in titles
    assert all(issue.step == 6 for issue in issues)

    output.write_text("no es una carpeta")
    issues = validation.validate_build(_config(tmp_path, output_dir=str(output)))
    assert any("no es una carpeta" in issue.message for issue in issues)


def test_validate_until_uses_passwordless_kiosk_policy(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    _fake_environment(monkeypatch)
    kiosk = _config(tmp_path, password="")
    assert validation.validate_until(1, kiosk) == []

    desktop = _config(tmp_path, project_type="office", password="")
    issues = validation.validate_until(1, desktop)
    assert issues and all(issue.step == 1 for issue in issues)
    assert validation.validate_until(0, kiosk) == []

==> ./tests/test_hardware_modules.py <==
"""Pruebas de los módulos de hardware del motor Fedora Desktop (Etapa 5)."""

import pytest

from core.desktops import (
    DESKTOP_ENVIRONMENTS,
    DesktopPackageError,
    KIOSK_ONLY_ENTRIES,
    UnknownHardwareModuleError,
    get_hardware_module,
    hardware_module_keys,
    list_hardware_modules,
    resolve_desktop_packages,
    resolve_hardware_packages,
)


def _entries(sections):
    return [entry for _title, entries in sections for entry in entries]


def test_catalogo_de_modulos_tiene_los_modulos_esperados():
    assert hardware_module_keys() == (
        "graphics",
        "wifi",
        "touchpad",
        "wifi_live_assistant",
    )
    names = {key for key, _name, _description in list_hardware_modules()}
    assert names == set(hardware_module_keys())
    assert get_hardware_module("graphics")["enabled"] is True


def test_modulo_desconocido_se_rechaza():
    with pytest.raises(UnknownHardwareModuleError):
        get_hardware_module("sonido")


def test_resolve_hardware_paquetes_ignora_modulos_no_solicitados():
    assert resolve_hardware_packages("gnome") == []


def test_resolve_hardware_grupos_wifi_y_graphics():
    entries = resolve_hardware_packages("kde", modules=["wifi", "graphics"])
    assert "NetworkManager-wifi" in entries
    assert "mesa-dri-drivers" in entries
    assert "libinput" not in entries


def test_resolve_hardware_wifi_live_assistant_activa_red():
    entries = resolve_hardware_packages("gnome", wifi_live_assistant=True)
    assert "NetworkManager-wifi" in entries
    assert "wpa_supplicant" in entries
    assert "linux-firmware" in entries


def test_resolve_hardware_deduplica_y_rechaza_paquetes_invalidos():
    entries = resolve_hardware_packages("xfce", modules=["wifi", "wifi"])
    assert entries.count("NetworkManager-wifi") == 1
    with pytest.raises(UnknownHardwareModuleError):
        resolve_hardware_packages("gnome", modules=["not-a-valid-module"])


def test_resolve_hardware_no_incluye_componentes_de_kiosco():
    entries = resolve_hardware_packages("kde", modules=["graphics", "wifi"])
    assert not (set(entries) & KIOSK_ONLY_ENTRIES)


def test_resolve_desktop_packages_integra_modulos_de_hardware():
    sections = resolve_desktop_packages("gnome", hardware_modules=["wifi"])
    entries = _entries(sections)
    assert "NetworkManager-wifi" in entries
    assert "mesa-dri-drivers" in entries

    sections = resolve_desktop_packages("gnome", wifi_live_assistant=True)
    entries = _entries(sections)
    assert "NetworkManager-wifi" in entries
    assert "wpa_supplicant" in entries


def test_resolve_desktop_packages_sin_modulos_no_agrega_hardware():
    base = resolve_desktop_packages("gnome")
    flat = _entries(base)
    assert "mesa-dri-drivers" in flat
    assert "NetworkManager-wifi" in flat
    assert not any(
        entry.startswith("Hardware y compatibilidad") for entry in flat
    )


def test_resolve_desktop_packages_rechaza_modulos_desconocidos():
    with pytest.raises(UnknownHardwareModuleError):
        resolve_desktop_packages("gnome", hardware_modules=["cage"])


def test_kickstart_del_engine_aplica_modulos_de_hardware(tmp_path):
    from core.desktops import DesktopKickstartGenerator

    generator = DesktopKickstartGenerator(
        {
            "desktop_environment": "gnome",
            "desktop_user": "tester",
            "password_hash": "$6$testsalt$" + "x" * 86,
            "hardware_modules": ["wifi"],
            "wifi_live_assistant": True,
        }
    )
    kickstart = generator.render()
    assert "NetworkManager-wifi" in kickstart
    assert "mesa-dri-drivers" in kickstart
    assert "cage" not in kickstart

==> ./tests/test_history.py <==
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from core.history import BackupManager, BuildHistory, redact_config


def test_redaction_is_recursive():
    value = redact_config({"password": "abc", "nested": {"ssh_token": "xyz", "ok": 2}})
    assert value["password"] == "[REDACTED]"
    assert value["nested"]["ssh_token"] == "[REDACTED]"
    assert value["nested"]["ok"] == 2


def test_history_lifecycle_and_hash(tmp_path):
    iso = tmp_path / "demo.iso"
    iso.write_bytes(b"iso-fixture")
    history = BuildHistory(tmp_path / "history.sqlite3")
    build_id = history.start({"iso_name": "Demo", "password": "should-not-persist"})
    history.finish(build_id, "GENERATED", iso)
    row = history.get(build_id)
    assert row["status"] == "GENERATED"
    assert row["iso_sha256"]
    assert "should-not-persist" not in row["config_json"]
    assert history.list()[0]["build_id"] == build_id


def test_backup_verify_restore_and_tamper(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / "project"
    source.mkdir()
    (source / "README.md").write_text("hello", encoding="utf-8")
    (source / "nested").mkdir()
    (source / "nested" / "data.txt").write_text("world", encoding="utf-8")
    manager = BackupManager(tmp_path / "backups")
    result = manager.create(source, "test")
    assert result["files"] == 2
    assert manager.verify(result["path"])["valid"]
    restored = tmp_path / "restored"
    manager.restore(result["path"], restored)
    assert (restored / "README.md").read_text() == "hello"
    assert (restored / "nested" / "data.txt").read_text() == "world"
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        manager.restore(result["path"], restored)


def test_backup_rejects_traversal(tmp_path):
    import tarfile
    archive = tmp_path / "bad.tar.gz"
    with tarfile.open(archive, "w:gz") as tar:
        import io
        data = b"bad"
        info = tarfile.TarInfo("../escape")
        info.size = len(data)
        tar.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(data))
    manager = BackupManager(tmp_path / "backups")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        manager.verify(archive)

==> ./tests/test_iso_lab.py <==
from core.iso.iso_inspector import inspect_iso_metadata
from core.kickstart.validator import validate_kickstart_content
from core.validation.iso_validator import validate_iso_package


def test_iso_inspector_reports_missing_file(tmp_path):
    result = inspect_iso_metadata(str(tmp_path / "missing.iso"))
    assert "error" in result


def test_iso_inspector_hashes_file_without_claiming_boot(tmp_path):
    image = tmp_path / "fixture.iso"
    image.write_bytes(b"not-a-real-iso" * 100)
    result = inspect_iso_metadata(str(image))
    assert len(result["sha256"]) == 64
    assert result["has_efi"] is False
    assert result["has_isolinux"] is False


def test_kickstart_validator_detects_missing_required_sections():
    issues = validate_kickstart_content("text\n")
    assert any(kind == "error" for kind, _ in issues)


def test_iso_validation_missing_path_is_error(tmp_path):
    result = validate_iso_package(str(tmp_path / "none.iso"))
    assert result["exists"] is False
    assert result["errors"]


def test_report_marks_qemu_unavailable_and_escapes_html():
    from core.validation.report_generator import generate_txt_report, generate_html_report
    data = {"path": "<script>alert(1)</script>", "qemu": {"bios": {"status": "NOT_AVAILABLE", "boot_markers": []}}}
    assert "BIOS: NOT_AVAILABLE" in generate_txt_report(data)
    assert "&lt;script&gt;" in generate_html_report(data)

==> ./tests/test_process_runner.py <==
"""Pruebas del ejecutor modular de procesos de la etapa 4."""

import subprocess
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from core.processes.runner import must, run_command, terminate_process_tree


def test_run_command_is_noninteractive_and_captures_output(monkeypatch):
    expected = SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="ok", stderr="")
    calls = {}

    def fake_run(cmd, **kwargs):
        calls["cmd"] = cmd
        calls.update(kwargs)
        return expected

    monkeypatch.setattr(subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    assert run_command(["printf", "ok"]) is expected
    assert calls["capture_output"] is True
    assert calls["text"] is True
    assert calls["stdin"] is subprocess.DEVNULL


def test_must_returns_success_and_reports_failure_detail():
    success = SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="", stderr="")
    assert must(success, "ejecutar") is success

    failure = SimpleNamespace(returncode=2, stdout="", stderr="detalle")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="detalle"):
        must(failure, "ejecutar")


def test_terminate_process_tree_escalates_after_timeout(monkeypatch):
    class FakeProcess:
        pid = 123
        def __init__(self):
            self.signals = []
            self.wait_calls = 0
        def send_signal(self, sig):
            self.signals.append(sig)
        def wait(self, timeout):
            self.wait_calls += 1
            if self.wait_calls == 1:
                raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired("fake", timeout)
            return 0

    import core.processes.runner as runner
    process = FakeProcess()
    monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "signal_process_group", lambda proc, sig: proc.send_signal(sig))
    terminate_process_tree(process, timeout=0.01)
    assert len(process.signals) == 2

==> ./tests/test_profile_security.py <==
import pytest
from core.profiles.manager import save_profile, load_profile, delete_profile, export_profile, import_profile


def test_profile_crud_import_export(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "profiles"
    save_profile("custom-one", {"profile_name": "Custom"}, root)
    assert load_profile("custom-one", root)["schema_version"] == 1
    exported = export_profile("custom-one", tmp_path / "export.json", root)
    import_profile(exported, "custom-two", root)
    assert load_profile("custom-two", root)["profile_name"] == "Custom"
    assert delete_profile("custom-two", root)


def test_profile_path_traversal_rejected(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        save_profile("../../outside", {}, tmp_path)

"""Pruebas unitarias para el módulo de seguridad (Etapa 6)."""

import pytest

from core.security.allowlist import is_package_allowed, validate_packages_list
from core.security.rpm_verifier import verify_rpm_signature
from core.security.credentials import password_hash


def test_allowlist_packages():
    assert is_package_allowed("firefox") is True
    assert is_package_allowed("telnet") is False  # en bloqueados
    assert is_package_allowed("malicious;rm -rf") is False


def test_validate_packages_list():
    res = validate_packages_list("firefox, telnet, htop, bad-pkg;inject")
    assert "firefox" in res["allowed"]
    assert "telnet" in res["blocked"]
    assert "bad-pkg;inject" in res["invalid"]


def test_rpm_verifier_nonexistent():
    res = verify_rpm_signature("/nonexistent/package.rpm")
    assert res["signed"] is False
    assert "error" in res


def test_password_hash_accepts_short_initial_password():
    value = password_hash("abc123")
    assert value.startswith("$6$")


def test_password_hash_rejects_empty_and_control_characters():
    for value in ("", "clave\ninyectada"):
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
            password_hash(value)


def test_rpm_verifier_requires_trusted_signature(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import core.security.rpm_verifier as verifier
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/usr/bin/rpmkeys")
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(
        returncode=0, stdout="package.rpm: digests signatures OK", stderr=""))
    result = verifier.verify_rpm_signature("package.rpm")
    assert result["verified"] is True
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(
        returncode=0, stdout="package.rpm: digests OK (GPG) NOKEY", stderr=""))
    result = verifier.verify_rpm_signature("package.rpm")
    assert result["verified"] is False


def test_rpm_verifier_classifies_unsigned_with_valid_digests(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import core.security.rpm_verifier as verifier
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/usr/bin/rpmkeys")
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(
        returncode=0, stdout="package.rpm: digests OK", stderr=""))
    result = verifier.verify_rpm_signature("package.rpm")
    assert result["unsigned"] is True
    assert result["digest_ok"] is True
    assert result["verified"] is False


def test_rpm_verifier_rejects_invalid_signature_even_if_unsigned_allowed(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import core.security.rpm_verifier as verifier
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/usr/bin/rpmkeys")
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(
        returncode=1, stdout="package.rpm: digests OK; signatures NOT OK", stderr=""))
    result = verifier.verify_rpm_signature("package.rpm")
    assert result["unsigned"] is False
    assert result["verified"] is False


def test_rpm_verifier_classifies_spanish_digest_output_as_unsigned(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import core.security.rpm_verifier as verifier
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/usr/bin/rpmkeys")
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(
        returncode=0,
        stdout=("Header SHA256 resumen: OK\nPayload SHA256 resumen: OK"),
        stderr=""))
    result = verifier.verify_rpm_signature("package.rpm")
    assert result["unsigned"] is True
    assert result["digest_ok"] is True
    assert result["verified"] is False


def test_rpm_verifier_rejects_spanish_bad_digest_output(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import core.security.rpm_verifier as verifier
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/usr/bin/rpmkeys")
    monkeypatch.setattr(verifier.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(
        returncode=1,
        stdout=("Header SHA256 resumen: OK\nPayload SHA256 resumen: BAD"),
        stderr=""))
    result = verifier.verify_rpm_signature("package.rpm")
    assert result["digest_ok"] is False
    assert result["unsigned"] is False

==> ./tests/test_software_policy.py <==
"""Pruebas de las políticas compartidas de selección de software."""

import pytest

from core.software.policy import (
    GROUP_NAME_MAX,
    PACKAGE_NAME_MAX,
    SEARCH_QUERY_MAX,
    SoftwareSelectionError,
    normalize_group_entry,
    normalize_group_id,
    normalize_groups,
    normalize_package_entry,
    normalize_packages,
    normalize_search_query,
    validate_group_names,
    validate_package_names,
)


def test_package_policy_accepts_rpm_names_and_rejects_groups():
    result = validate_package_names("htop git gimp, @office")
    assert result == {
        "allowed": ["htop", "git", "gimp"],
        "blocked": [],
        "invalid": ["@office"],
    }


def test_package_policy_rejects_blocked_and_control_characters():
    result = validate_package_names(["telnet", "htop;rm", "paquete\nraro"])
    assert result["allowed"] == []
    assert result["blocked"] == ["telnet"]
    assert result["invalid"] == ["htop;rm", "paquete\nraro"]


def test_package_name_length_limit():
    name = "a" * PACKAGE_NAME_MAX
    assert normalize_package_entry(name) == name
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError, match="supera"):
        normalize_package_entry(name + "a")


def test_normalize_packages_deduplicates_and_reports_all_problems():
    assert normalize_packages("htop git htop") == ["htop", "git"]
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError) as error:
        normalize_packages("telnet htop;rm")
    message = str(error.value)
    assert "telnet" in message
    assert "htop;rm" in message
    assert "inválidos" in message


def test_group_policy_accepts_group_and_environment_entries():
    valid = validate_group_names(
        ["@office", "@^workstation-product-environment", "office"]
    )
    assert valid["allowed"] == [
        "@office",
        "@^workstation-product-environment",
    ]
    assert valid["invalid"] == ["office"]


def test_normalize_group_entry_variants():
    assert normalize_group_entry("office") == "@office"
    assert normalize_group_entry("@office") == "@office"
    assert normalize_group_entry("@^office") == "@^office"


def test_group_id_and_length_validation():
    assert normalize_group_id("office") == "office"
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError):
        normalize_group_id("@office")
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError):
        normalize_group_id("office grupo")
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError):
        normalize_group_id("a" * (GROUP_NAME_MAX + 1))


def test_normalize_groups_deduplicates_and_rejects_invalid_entries():
    assert normalize_groups("office @office @^workstation") == [
        "@office",
        "@^workstation",
    ]
    with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError, match="grupo"):
        normalize_groups(["@office", "@grupo con espacios"])


def test_search_query_accepts_unicode_and_rejects_unsafe_text():
    assert normalize_search_query("  editor de imágenes  ") == "editor de imágenes"
    assert normalize_search_query("") == ""
    for query in (
        "-peligro",
        "foo -bar",
        "linea\nnueva",
        "nul\x00",
        "x" * (SEARCH_QUERY_MAX + 1),
    ):
        with pytest.raises(SoftwareSelectionError):
            normalize_search_query(query)

==> ./tests/test_usb.py <==
"""Tests del modulo core/usb.py: enumeracion de discos y progreso de dd."""

import json
import subprocess

import pytest

from core import usb


def _lsblk_result(stdout="", returncode=0, stderr=""):
    return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
        args=["lsblk"], returncode=returncode, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr
    )


def _disk(name, **overrides):
    """Disco USB tipico tal como lo reporta lsblk -J -b."""
    device = {
        "name": name,
        "path": f"/dev/{name}",
        "type": "disk",
        "size": 32 * 1024 ** 3,
        "rm": True,
        "ro": False,
        "hotplug": False,
        "tran": "usb",
        "model": "USB Disk",
        "serial": "SN-1",
        "maj:min": "8:16",
        "mountpoints": [],
        "children": [],
    }
    device.update(overrides)
    return device


def _devices_json(*devices):
    return json.dumps({"blockdevices": list(devices)})


def _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, stdout="", returncode=0, stderr=""):
    monkeypatch.setattr(usb.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/usr/bin/lsblk")
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        usb.subprocess,
        "run",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: _lsblk_result(stdout, returncode, stderr),
    )


# -- Utilidades ------------------------------------------------------------


def test_is_system_mount():
    # Raiz y subarboles del sistema.
    for mount in ("/", "/boot", "/boot/efi", "/var", "/var/tmp", "/home/x",
                  "/etc", "/opt/y", "/root", "/srv", "/usr"):
        assert usb._is_system_mount(mount) is True, mount

    # Puntos de montaje tipicos de medios extraibles.
    for mount in ("/media/usb", "/mnt", "/run/media/user/KINGSTON"):
        assert usb._is_system_mount(mount) is False, mount

    # Prefijo similar pero distinto no cuenta.
    assert usb._is_system_mount("/bootstrap") is False
    assert usb._is_system_mount("/homey") is False


def test_size_gib():
    assert usb.size_gib(1024 ** 3) == 1.0
    assert usb.size_gib(0) == 0.0
    assert usb.size_gib(None) == 0.0
    assert usb.size_gib("no-numero") == 0.0
    assert usb.size_gib("1073741824") == 1.0


def test_mountpoints_normalization():
    assert usb._mountpoints({"mountpoints": ["/a", ""]}) == ["/a"]
    assert usb._mountpoints({"mountpoints": "/b"}) == ["/b"]
    assert usb._mountpoints({"mountpoints": None}) == []
    assert usb._mountpoints({}) == []
    assert usb._mountpoints({"mountpoints": ["", None]}) == []


# -- list_usb_disks --------------------------------------------------------


def test_list_usb_disks_requires_lsblk(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(usb.shutil, "which", lambda name: None)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="lsblk"):
        usb.list_usb_disks()


def test_list_usb_disks_raises_on_lsblk_failure(monkeypatch):
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, returncode=1, stderr="lsblk: error fatal")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="lsblk fallo"):
        usb.list_usb_disks()


def test_list_usb_disks_raises_on_bad_json(monkeypatch):
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, stdout="esto no es json")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="interpretar la salida"):
        usb.list_usb_disks()


def test_list_usb_disks_keeps_only_removable_usb_disks(monkeypatch):
    devices = [
        _disk("sda", rm=False, hotplug=False, tran="sata", model="Disco SATA"),
        _disk("sdb"),
        _disk("sdc", ro=True),
        {"name": "sdb1", "type": "part", "path": "/dev/sdb1"},
    ]
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(*devices))

    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert [disk["path"] for disk in disks] == ["/dev/sdb"]
    assert disks[0]["model"] == "USB Disk"
    assert disks[0]["serial"] == "SN-1"
    assert disks[0]["major_minor"] == "8:16"
    assert disks[0]["removable"] is True
    assert disks[0]["tran"] == "usb"
    assert disks[0]["size"] == 32 * 1024 ** 3


def test_list_usb_disks_excludes_system_disks(monkeypatch):
    devices = [
        # Host arrancado desde USB: raiz en el propio dispositivo.
        _disk("sdd", mountpoints=["/"]),
        # Disco del sistema con /boot/efi en una particion hija.
        _disk(
            "sde",
            children=[
                {
                    "name": "sde1",
                    "path": "/dev/sde1",
                    "mountpoints": ["/boot/efi"],
                }
            ],
        ),
        _disk("sdb"),
    ]
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(*devices))

    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert [disk["path"] for disk in disks] == ["/dev/sdb"]


def test_list_usb_disks_keeps_data_disks_with_media_mounts(monkeypatch):
    # Un USB con datos montados en /run/media SI se ofrece: es el caso de
    # uso normal (se avisa al usuario antes de borrar).
    usb_disk = _disk(
        "sdb",
        children=[
            {
                "name": "sdb1",
                "path": "/dev/sdb1",
                "mountpoints": ["/run/media/user/KINGSTON"],
            }
        ],
    )
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(usb_disk))

    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert [disk["path"] for disk in disks] == ["/dev/sdb"]


def test_list_usb_disks_merges_child_mountpoints(monkeypatch):
    usb_disk = _disk(
        "sdb",
        children=[
            {
                "name": "sdb1",
                "path": "/dev/sdb1",
                "mountpoints": ["/run/media/user/KINGSTON"],
            },
            {"name": "sdb2", "path": "/dev/sdb2", "mountpoints": None},
        ],
    )
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(usb_disk))

    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert len(disks) == 1
    assert "/run/media/user/KINGSTON" in disks[0]["mountpoints"]
    assert [child["path"] for child in disks[0]["children"]] == [
        "/dev/sdb1",
        "/dev/sdb2",
    ]
    assert disks[0]["children"][0]["mountpoints"] == [
        "/run/media/user/KINGSTON"
    ]


def test_list_usb_disks_accepts_usb_without_removable_flag(monkeypatch):
    # Algunos pendrives reportan rm=0; si TRAN dice usb se acepta igual.
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(_disk("sdb", rm=False, hotplug=False)))
    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert len(disks) == 1
    assert disks[0]["removable"] is False


def test_list_usb_disks_accepts_hotplug_without_usb_transport(monkeypatch):
    # Discos NVMe externos: sin TRAN pero marcados hotplug.
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(_disk("sdb", hotplug=True, tran="")))
    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert len(disks) == 1
    assert disks[0]["removable"] is True


def test_list_usb_disks_path_fallback_to_dev_name(monkeypatch):
    device = _disk("sdb")
    device.pop("path")
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(device))
    disks = usb.list_usb_disks()
    assert disks[0]["path"] == "/dev/sdb"


def test_list_usb_disks_skips_non_dev_paths(monkeypatch):
    _patch_lsblk(monkeypatch, _devices_json(_disk("sdb", path="/mapper/loop0")))
    assert usb.list_usb_disks() == []


# -- USBWriterWorker._handle_output ----------------------------------------


def test_handle_output_progress_and_log_gate():
    worker = usb.USBWriterWorker("/tmp/fake.iso", "/dev/null")
    logs = []
    progress = []
    worker.log_signal.connect(logs.append)
    worker.progress_signal.connect(progress.append)

    iso_size = 10 * 1024 * 1024

    # Primer reporte de dd: "N bytes copied".
    last = worker._handle_output("1048576 bytes copied", iso_size, -10)
    assert progress == [10]
    assert last == 10
    assert len(logs) == 1

    last = worker._handle_output("2097152 bytes copied", iso_size, last)
    assert progress == [10, 20]
    assert last == 20
    assert len(logs) == 2

    # Menos de 5 puntos mas: mueve la barra pero no escribe log.
    # (2359296 bytes = 2.25 MiB = 22% de la ISO de 10 MiB.)
    last = worker._handle_output("2359296 bytes copied", iso_size, last)
    assert progress == [10, 20, 22]
    assert last == 20
    assert len(logs) == 2

    # El progreso queda en 99% hasta el final (el 100 lo emite run()).
    last = worker._handle_output("999999999999 bytes copied", iso_size, last)
    assert progress[-1] == 99
    assert last == 99

    # Lineas normales van al log y no tocan el progreso.
    frozen_progress = list(progress)
    last = worker._handle_output("ERROR: /dev/sdb1 sigue montado", iso_size, last)
    assert logs[-1] == "ERROR: /dev/sdb1 sigue montado"
    assert progress == frozen_progress
    assert last == 99

    # Lineas vacias no hacen nada.
    assert worker._handle_output("   ", iso_size, last) == last
    assert worker._handle_output("", iso_size, last) == last
    assert progress == frozen_progress


def test_validate_device_snapshot_accepts_same_device():
    current = {"major_minor": "8:16", "size": 32, "serial": "SN-1", "model": "USB"}
    usb._validate_device_snapshot(current, dict(current))


def test_validate_device_snapshot_rejects_replaced_device():
    current = {"major_minor": "8:32", "size": 32, "serial": "SN-2", "model": "USB"}
    original = {"major_minor": "8:16", "size": 32, "serial": "SN-1", "model": "USB"}
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="major_minor"):
        usb._validate_device_snapshot(current, original)


def test_validate_device_snapshot_requires_kernel_identity():
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="MAJ:MIN"):
        usb._validate_device_snapshot({"size": 32}, {"size": 32})


# -- USBWriterWorker._build_write_script ----------------------------------------


def _writer_with_snapshot(snapshot):
    return usb.USBWriterWorker("/tmp/fake.iso", "/dev/sdb", device_snapshot=snapshot)


def test_build_write_script_trims_lsblk_output():
    worker = _writer_with_snapshot(
        {
            "major_minor": "8:16",
            "size": 32 * 1024 ** 3,
            "serial": "SN-1",
            "model": "USB",
        }
    )
    script = worker._build_write_script(
        "/tmp/fake.iso",
        "/dev/sdb",
        worker.device_snapshot,
        [],
        [],
        32 * 1024 ** 3,
    )
    assert any(
        "actual_mm=$(lsblk -dn -o MAJ:MIN -- /dev/sdb | tr -d '[:space:]')" in line
        for line in script
    )
    assert any(
        "ERROR: identidad MAJ:MIN cambiada" in line for line in script
    )


def test_build_write_script_identity_mismatch_exits_20():
    worker = _writer_with_snapshot(
        {"major_minor": "8:32", "size": 32 * 1024 ** 3}
    )
    script = worker._build_write_script(
        "/tmp/fake.iso",
        "/dev/sdb",
        worker.device_snapshot,
        [],
        [],
        32 * 1024 ** 3,
    )
    joined = "\n".join(script)
    assert "ERROR: identidad MAJ:MIN cambiada" in joined
    assert "exit 20" in joined


def test_build_write_script_serial_mismatch_exits_22():
    worker = _writer_with_snapshot(
        {"major_minor": "8:16", "size": 32 * 1024 ** 3, "serial": "SN-2"}
    )
    script = worker._build_write_script(
        "/tmp/fake.iso",
        "/dev/sdb",
        worker.device_snapshot,
        [],
        [],
        32 * 1024 ** 3,
    )
    joined = "\n".join(script)
    assert "ERROR: numero de serie cambiado" in joined
    assert "exit 22" in joined


def test_build_write_script_mounted_exits_with_constant():
    worker = _writer_with_snapshot({"major_minor": "8:16", "size": 32 * 1024 ** 3})
    script = worker._build_write_script(
        "/tmp/fake.iso",
        "/dev/sdb",
        worker.device_snapshot,
        ["/dev/sdb1"],
        [],
        32 * 1024 ** 3,
    )
    joined = "\n".join(script)
    assert "ERROR: /dev/sdb1 sigue montado" in joined
    assert "exit 3" in joined


def test_build_write_script_ok_includes_dd_and_cmp():
    worker = _writer_with_snapshot({"major_minor": "8:16", "size": 32 * 1024 ** 3})
    script = worker._build_write_script(
        "/tmp/fake.iso",
        "/dev/sdb",
        worker.device_snapshot,
        [],
        [],
        32 * 1024 ** 3,
    )
    joined = "\n".join(script)
    assert "dd if=" in joined
    assert "of=/dev/sdb" in joined
    assert "cmp -n" in joined
